Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Each subproblem has a solution by the duality relation fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: We apply the factorization result principle to the quantum decomposition. By minimality, the element a^394 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 155-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
